  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在ROCKET方法中，将多选择背包问题（MCKP）的分配代价从权重空间Frobenius误差改为与输出重建目标一致的成本，是否能提升压缩后模型的保真度。

Method: 提出ROCKET-ActCost，使用输出空间的激活重建误差作为MCKP分配的代价函数，与原有的权重空间Frobenius误差进行对比，在Qwen3-8B和Llama-3.2-1B模型上进行50%和20%的压缩实验。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B 50%压缩时，ROCKET-ActCost在8个零样本任务上平均准确率提升0.8个百分点（53.1% vs 52.3%），但WikiText困惑度上升16%（61.46 vs 52.98）；在Llama-3.2-1B 20%压缩时，两种方法结果几乎相同（准确率53.3% vs 53.5%，困惑度14.45 vs 14.66）。权重空间与输出空间误差高度相关（>0.99），导致分配策略差异小，效果有限。

Conclusion: 对齐分配代价与输出重建目标可在较高压缩比下轻微提升下游任务准确率，但以增加困惑度为代价；在低压缩比下影响极小，说明代价函数的选择在训练免费压缩中作用有限。

Abstract: Training-free compression methods for large language models (LLMs) often use calibration data to guide compression decisions. ROCKET, a recent method combining sparse-dictionary factorization with multi-choice knapsack problem (MCKP) allocation, derives its per-layer factorization from an output reconstruction objective but uses weight-space Frobenius error as the MCKP allocation cost. We investigate whether aligning the allocation cost with the output-space objective improves compressed model fidelity. On Qwen3-8B at 50\% compression, our ROCKET-ActCost achieves +0.8 percentage points higher average accuracy across 8 zero-shot benchmarks (53.1\% vs 52.3\%), but increases WikiText perplexity by 16\% (61.46 vs 52.98). This accuracy-perplexity tradeoff reveals that different allocation objectives favor different downstream metrics. The high correlation ($>$0.99) between weight-space and output-space errors limits allocation divergence, explaining the modest effect size. On Llama-3.2-1B at 20\% compression, the two methods produce near-identical results (53.3\% vs 53.5\% accuracy, 14.45 vs 14.66 PPL), suggesting that the effect of the cost function is minor at lower compression ratios.

</details>


### [113] [SHIFT: Gate-Modulated Activation Steering for Knowledge Conflict Mitigation in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27786)
*Ruochang Li,Pengcheng Huang,Zhenghao Liu,Yukun Yan,Huiyuan Xie,Yu Gu,Ge Yu,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SHIFT的新框架，通过可学习的门控机制调节LLM内部激活，以解决检索增强生成（RAG）中的知识冲突问题，仅需优化不到0.01%的可训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成（RAG）中存在检索上下文与模型参数化知识之间的冲突，现有基于神经元编辑的方法可能破坏模型整体能力，因此需要一种更安全、可控的解决方案。

Method: 将神经元级修改转化为可学习的门控调制，引入轻量级门模块，在保持主干模型冻结的情况下动态调节内部表示，以自适应地融合上下文和参数化知识。

Result: 在六个数据集上的实验表明，SHIFT在解决知识冲突方面优于多种基线方法，且仅需训练极少参数。

Conclusion: SHIFT提供了一种高效、低干扰的方式，使LLM能够在RAG中更好地平衡外部上下文与内部知识，提升生成准确性的同时保留模型原有能力。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) enhances LLMs by incorporating external knowledge to support response generation. However, conflicts between retrieved context and parametric knowledge have emerged as a critical challenge in RAG systems. To mitigate such conflicts, numerous studies have attempted to identify and edit knowledge-related internal neurons, aiming to improve the ability of LLMs to rely on contextual evidence during generation. However, these neuron-level approaches may introduce unintended cascading effects that compromise the general capabilities of LLMs, as the modified neurons are often entangled with broader model behaviors and functionalities. In this paper, we introduce SHIFT, a novel framework that reformulates neuron-level modification as learnable gate modulation, allowing LLMs to adaptively regulate internal activations for knowledge conflict resolution. Technically, our SHIFT equips LLMs with a lightweight gate module and optimizes fewer than 0.01% trainable parameters while keeping the backbone model frozen. During generation, the gate module adjusts the model's internal representations to adaptively leverage contextual and parametric knowledge. Extensive experiments on six datasets validate the effectiveness of our SHIFT in comparison with various competing baselines. All datasets and code are available at https://github.com/OpenBMB/SHIFT.

</details>


### [114] [NLL-Guided Full-Attention Layer Selection for Training-Free Sliding-Window Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27791)
*Qiong Tang,Xiangkun Hu,Xiangyang Liu,Yiran Chen,Yunfan Shao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的NLL引导层选择方法，用于混合注意力模型中选择关键层使用全注意力机制，在减少计算开销的同时保持甚至提升长上下文任务的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有混合注意力模型中如何选择哪些层应保留全注意力的问题，现有方法依赖固定周期或启发式规则，可能无法准确反映对下游任务性能的影响。

Method: 通过计算当某一层从全注意力切换为滑动窗口注意力时答案标记的负对数似然（NLL）退化程度，来衡量该层的重要性，从而决定是否保留其全注意力机制。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B和LongMemEval上，仅使用1/4的全注意力层即达到64.6%的准确率，接近使用1/2全注意力层的周期性基线（65.0%），且显著优于其他基线方法最多达26.4个百分点；校准时间仅需约15分钟。

Conclusion: NLL引导的层选择是一种高效、有效的训练-free方法，能够在显著降低计算成本的同时维持高性能，推动了长上下文大模型部署中的效率-精度帕累托前沿。

Abstract: Hybrid attention models that mix full and sliding-window attention across layers offer a promising approach to efficient long-context inference, but the critical question of \emph{which layers} should retain full attention remains unsolved. Existing methods use either fixed periodic patterns or attention-based heuristics that may not capture what matters for downstream accuracy. We propose NLL-guided layer selection, a training-free method that directly measures each layer's importance by computing the negative log-likelihood degradation on answer tokens when that layer uses sliding-window instead of full attention. On LongMemEval with Qwen3-4B, our method achieves 64.6\% accuracy using only 1/4 full-attention layers, matching the 1/2-FA periodic baseline (65.0\%) while halving the computational budget. NLL-guided selection outperforms the SWAA-reported periodic 1/4-FA baseline by 10.4 percentage points and a matched LightTransfer-style baseline by 26.4 percentage points. De-confounding analysis shows the signal is consistent with long-range attention needs rather than generic layer sensitivity. The method requires only $\sim$15 minutes of one-time calibration, advancing the efficiency-accuracy Pareto frontier for long-context LLM deployment.

</details>


### [115] [Position Bias Correction is Insufficient for One-Pass Attention Sorting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27793)
*Qiong Tang,Xiangkun Hu,Xiangyang Liu,Yiran Chen,Yunfan Shao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Debiased One-Pass Attention Sorting尝试通过校正位置偏差来实现单次排序，但在实验中未能完全匹配迭代排序的效果，表明位置偏差校正不足以完全替代重复重排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文语言模型存在位置偏差问题，中间位置信息利用不足，而现有的Attention Sorting方法因多轮排序导致部署成本高，因此需要更高效的解决方案。

Method: 提出Debiased One-Pass Attention Sorting，通过从低注意力文档中估计每提示的位置偏差曲线，并用减法或除法校正原始注意力得分，以实现单次排序。

Result: 在LLaMA-2-7B-32K-Instruct上，去偏校正与未校正的单次排序结果相同（94.83%包含准确率）；在YaRN-Llama-2-7b-64k上，准确率提升8.67个百分点，但仍落后迭代排序14.84个百分点，仅缩小了37%的差距。

Conclusion: 位置偏差校正是不够的，重复重排序带来了超出偏差校正之外的额外好处。

Abstract: Long-context language models suffer from position bias, where information in middle positions is underutilized. Attention Sorting addresses this by iteratively reordering documents based on attention patterns, but its multiple sort-and-generate cycles increase deployment cost. We hypothesize that position bias is the primary bottleneck and propose Debiased One-Pass Attention Sorting, which estimates a per-prompt position-bias curve from the low-attention majority of documents and uses it to correct raw attention scores (via subtraction or division) to enable single-pass sorting. Our experiments on two models refute this hypothesis in the tested setting: on LLaMA-2-7B-32K-Instruct, debiasing produces identical results to uncalibrated single-pass sorting (94.83\% containment accuracy), while on YaRN-Llama-2-7b-64k, debiasing improves accuracy by 8.67 percentage points but remains 14.84pp behind iterative sorting, closing only 37\% of the gap. These results suggest that position-bias correction is insufficient to match iterative sorting, and that repeated reordering provides additional benefits beyond bias correction.

</details>


### [116] [Learning Complementary Action Modeling from Automotive Maintenance Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27808)
*Jiaqi Wu,Bai Li,Jochen Hartmann,Martin Gaedke,Sander Stuijk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了“互补动作建模”（CAM）任务，旨在通过修改维护指令中的动作短语来识别或生成其程序对应项，并强调细微词汇线索在建模程序关联中的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于微小的词汇变化可能完全改变指令的程序含义，现有句子相似性或同义改写方法无法准确捕捉这种变化，因此需要专门建模此类现象。

Method: 基于德语汽车维护数据集，采用候选匹配和可控的Seq2Seq生成方法，从检索、重叠度和人工评估三方面分析互补动作建模的效果。

Result: 实验结果表明，互补维护指令应被视为由细微词汇线索决定的程序关联，而不应被当作普通的句子相似性或同义改写问题处理。

Conclusion: 互补动作建模需关注动作短语级别的细粒度变化，传统句子相似性模型不足以准确识别或生成具有程序互补性的指令。

Abstract: A minute lexical variation can reverse the procedural meaning of an instruction even when the rest of the sentence remains unchanged. In automotive maintenance instructions, this pattern often appears when an action phrase turns an instruction into its procedural counterpart. The entities, modifiers, and surrounding context remain largely invariant, while the action phrase determines the procedural relation. We define this task as Complementary Action Modeling (CAM). Given a maintenance instruction, the goal is to identify or generate its procedural counterpart by modifying the action phrase while preserving the remaining sentence context. This task focuses on three aspects: distinguishing complementarity from surface similarity, controlling generation at the action-phrase level, and evaluating relational correctness using retrieval, overlap-based, and human evaluation. Using a German automotive maintenance dataset, we examine these questions through candidate matching and controlled Seq2Seq generation. The results show that complementary maintenance instructions are best modeled as procedural associations grounded in subtle lexical cues. They should therefore not be treated as ordinary cases of sentence similarity or synonym-based paraphrasing.

</details>


### [117] [A Study of Temporal Fusion Strategies for Named Entity Recognition in Historical Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27881)
*Emanuela Boros*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何通过轻量级融合策略将时间元数据嵌入到基于Transformer的命名实体识别模型中，以提升历史文本中实体识别的时序泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史文本中的命名实体识别面临实体随时间变化的表面形式和显著性漂移问题，而现有语言模型在处理时序信息尤其是历时语境方面能力有限。

Method: 采用绝对和相对时间表示，通过早期或晚期融合机制（如交叉注意力、适配器和拼接）将其结构化地注入Transformer架构中，并在法语和德语历史数据集上进行实验。

Result: 实验结果表明，晚期融合策略在早期和噪声较多的历史时期表现出更鲁棒且更具时序泛化性的性能。

Conclusion: 将时间信息通过晚期融合方式引入模型，能有效提升历史文本命名实体识别的准确性与时序适应性。

Abstract: Temporal variation poses a unique challenge for named entity recognition (NER) in historical texts, where entities drift in surface form and salience across time. While language models (LMs) have made progress in various NLP tasks, their ability to reason about temporality, especially in diachronic contexts, remains limited or at least, questionable. In this paper, we systematically study how temporal metadata can be structurally embedded into NER models using a range of lightweight fusion strategies. We experiment with both absolute and relative temporal representations, injected into Transformer-based architectures via early or late fusion mechanisms such as cross-attention, adapters, and concatenation. Our evaluations on French and German historical datasets reveal that late fusion strategies yield more robust and temporally generalisable performance, particularly in early and noisy periods.

</details>


### [118] [Triadic Werewolf: A Jester Role for Multi-Hop Theory of Mind in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27909)
*Avni Mittal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过引入第三方角色“小丑”扩展了狼人游戏，揭示了大型语言模型在多智能体推理中的局限性，尤其是在处理三方激励结构时的表现差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的心智理论评估通常使用双人社交推理游戏，无法充分测试模型对对手动机的模拟能力，因此需要更复杂的博弈结构来揭示模型的真实推理水平。

Method: 在狼人游戏中引入具有反转效用函数的第三方角色“小丑”，设计包含三个对立效用函数的三人博弈场景，并在GPT-4.1、DeepSeek-V3.1和Llama-3.3-70B上进行60局游戏测试，比较不同模型在有无自我学习情况下的表现。

Result: 实验显示，小丑在游戏中获胜60-70%，而狼人从未超过20%；GPT-4.1控制的狼人在60-70%的对局中首日就将小丑投票出局，这是一种自我挫败行为；自我学习对DeepSeek和Llama有帮助，但降低了GPT-4.1的表现，且代价由村民承担；只有DeepSeek学会了看似可疑但不显故意的复杂策略。

Conclusion: 三元激励结构暴露了双人推理游戏无法发现的多智能体推理层次，现有大模型在跨多个对立动机进行推理方面存在显著差异和局限，表明当前心智理论评估需更复杂的互动场景以准确衡量模型的社会推理能力。

Abstract: Theory-of-mind evaluations of large language models typically use dyadic social-deduction games, where every observable cue points to a single hidden side, so a model with strong language priors can score well without ever simulating opponents' incentives. We extend the Werewolf game with a Jester, a third faction whose utility on peer suspicion is inverted because it wins by being voted out, so optimal play requires reasoning across three opposing utility functions. Across 60 games on GPT-4.1, DeepSeek-V3.1, and Llama-3.3-70B with Jester self-learning on and off, the Jester wins 60-70% of games while Werewolves never exceed 20%, and GPT-4.1 wolves vote the Jester out on day 1 in 60-70% of games, a strictly self-defeating action. Self-learning helps DeepSeek and Llama but hurts GPT-4.1, with the cost landing on Villagers rather than Werewolves. Only DeepSeek learns the subtle strategy of looking suspicious without looking intentionally suspicious, and it gains the most from the loop. Triadic incentive structure exposes a layer of multi-agent reasoning that dyadic deduction games leave invisible.

</details>


### [119] [VASAE: Naming SAE Dictionary Directions with Vocabulary-Aligned Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27941)
*Kairui Zhang,Ziwen Yu,Zahraa S. Abdallah,Martha Lewis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Vocabulary-Aligned Sparse Autoencoder (VASAE)，通过在训练中引入词汇对齐锚定，使稀疏自编码器的特征具有内在的词元名称，提升了对Transformer模型中学习特征的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的稀疏自编码器（SAE）虽然能有效分解Transformer残差流，但其学习到的特征通常缺乏与词表的直接关联，需依赖事后命名。本文旨在在训练过程中建立特征与词汇的内在联系。

Method: 提出VASAE方法，在SAE训练中引入词汇对齐锚定机制，将每个特征与其最近的词嵌入对应的词元字符串进行绑定，赋予特征内在的词元名称，并评估对齐得分。

Result: 在GPT-2-small上，0–10层约90%的特征实现了对齐（阈值0.8）；Llama-3.1-8B的浅层和中间层字典表现出强对齐性（浅层达92.8%），末层对齐有限；去均值后案例显示多数剩余名称与输入词相关。

Conclusion: 词汇对齐锚定能在不牺牲重建性能的前提下，有效连接学习特征与内在词元名称，增强字典的可解释性，是对事后解释方法的有益补充。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) provide useful decompositions of Transformer residual streams, but their learned features are usually named post hoc rather than directly connected to the Transformer's token vocabulary. We introduce Vocabulary-Aligned Sparse Autoencoder (VASAE), a method that trains SAE features under vocabulary-aligned anchoring and assigns each feature an intrinsic token name: the token string whose embedding is nearest to that feature. Without reducing reconstruction quality compared with a standard SAE, VASAE produces dictionaries with vocabulary-aligned features. Using a 0.8 cutoff on the nearest-token alignment score, dictionaries trained on GPT-2-small post-residual streams align about 90% of features in layers 0--10. In Llama-3.1-8B, representative shallow and middle-layer dictionaries contain strongly aligned features, including 92.8% in the shallow layer, while the representative final-layer dictionary shows limited alignment. After subtracting the sentence-level mean sparse code, case studies show that many remaining intrinsic token names are relevant to nearby input tokens. These results suggest that vocabulary-aligned anchoring can connect learned features to intrinsic token names during training, complementing post hoc interpretation of learned dictionaries.

</details>


### [120] [An Empirical Analysis of Factual Errors in Human-Written Text and its Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27959)
*Kazuma Iwamoto,Kazumasa Omura,Shotaro Ishihara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了人类写作文本中的事实错误检测（FED），提出了一个基于报纸文章修正的错误分类体系，并评估了大语言模型在合成和真实数据上的表现，发现即使高性能模型如GPT-5.4也仅达到52%的词级F1分数，表明该任务具有较高难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型的发展，研究多集中于检测模型生成的幻觉错误，而忽视了人类写作中事实错误的检测。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统分析人类写作中的典型错误类型并评估现有模型的能力。

Method: 通过分析报纸文章的修订内容构建人类诱发事实错误的分类体系，并基于该分类生成合成测试用例和使用真实修正数据，评估多种大语言模型在事实错误检测任务上的表现。

Result: 实验结果显示，即使是高性能的大语言模型（如GPT-5.4）在合成数据上的词级F1得分仅为52%，且存在特定类型的错误（如汉字误转、数量词分类错误）难以被检测。

Conclusion: 人类写作中的事实错误检测仍具挑战性，当前大语言模型在此类任务上表现有限，需针对特定错误类型设计更精细的检测方法。

Abstract: Factual Error Detection (FED), which is the task of identifying factually incorrect spans in a given text, has long been recognized as an important research problem. However, with the rapid rise of large language models (LLMs), research attention has shifted toward factual errors specific to LLM-generated text (hallucinations) and their detection. As a result, the detection of factual errors in human-written text has been relatively neglected. To address this gap, we first distill a taxonomy of human-induced factual errors by analyzing corrections of newspaper articles, a representative source of text that is guaranteed to be human-written and contains few grammatical errors. Our analysis revealed that there are characteristic categories such as kanji misconversions and numeral classifier errors, which are not focused in existing hallucination benchmarks. Based on the taxonomy, we then evaluate the FED capability of vanilla LLMs on synthesized realistic test cases and real corrections. Experimental results demonstrated that even high-performance LLMs such as GPT-5.4 achieved only word-level F1 score of 52% on the synthetic evaluation data, highlighting the task difficulty. Furthermore, a detailed analysis by detection difficulty revealed the current state of FED.

</details>


### [121] [From Black-Box to Clinical Insight: A Multi-Stage Explainable Framework for Speech-Based Cognitive Impairment Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27973)
*Yasaman Haghbin,Sina Rashidi,Ali Zolnour,Fatemeh Taherinezhad,Ali Fartoot,Hossein Azadmaleki,James M Noble,Maryam Dadkhah,Maryam Zolnoori*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种多阶段可解释性框架，将基于Transformer的语音认知障碍检测模型的预测结果转化为临床可理解的叙述，结合SHAP-token归因、理论驱动的语言特征和LLaMA-3.1-70B-Instruct的四阶段大语言模型推理流程，映射到词汇丰富性、句法复杂性、语义连贯性等四个认知语言维度，医生评估显示与患者认知特征高度一致，系统可用性评分82/100，具备良好临床集成潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于Transformer的语音认知障碍检测模型虽然有效，但缺乏临床可解释性，限制了其在实际医疗场景中的应用。需要一种能够将黑箱模型输出转化为临床医生可理解的、基于证据的解释框架。

Method: 构建了一个多阶段可解释性框架：首先利用SHAP方法进行token级归因分析；然后结合理论指导的言语特征（如词汇、句法、语义等）；最后通过一个四阶段的大语言模型（LLaMA-3.1-70B-Instruct）推理流程生成临床叙事。该框架基于SpeechCARE-Adaptive Gating Network模型（在NIA PREPARE基准上F1=72.11%），并将模型输出映射到四个认知-语言维度。

Result: 在70个分层的英语样本上进行的医生评估表明，生成的解释与患者的认知特征高度一致；系统可用性量表（SUS）得分为82/100，表明该框架具有较高的临床工作流集成潜力。

Conclusion: 所提出的多阶段可解释性框架成功地将黑箱Transformer模型的预测转化为临床上可理解的、基于语言学证据的认知评估叙述，提升了模型的透明度和临床可信度，为AI辅助认知障碍筛查的落地应用提供了重要支持。

Abstract: Speech-based cognitive impairment detection offers a noninvasive, accessible alternative to costly biomarker assays, yet transformer-based models remain clinically uninterpretable. We propose a multi-stage explainability framework that translates black-box transformer predictions into clinically grounded narratives by integrating SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP)-based token attribution, theory-informed linguistic features, and a four-stage LLM reasoning pipeline using LLaMA-3.1-70B-Instruct. Built on the SpeechCARE-Adaptive Gating Network multimodal screening model (F1 = 72.11% on the NIA PREPARE benchmark), the framework maps model outputs to four cognitive-linguistic dimensions, including lexical richness, syntactic complexity, and semantic coherence. Physician evaluation on 70 stratified English samples demonstrated strong alignment with patient-level cognitive profiles, and a System Usability Scale score of 82/100 indicated high potential for clinical workflow integration.

</details>


### [122] [ToxiREX: A Dataset on Toxic REasoning in ConteXt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27981)
*Stefan F. Schouten,Ilia Markov,Piek Vossen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ToxiREX是一个新的多语言、上下文相关的毒性推理数据集，包含六种语言的Reddit评论线程，采用系统化的毒性推理模式标注，支持隐式和上下文相关毒性的识别与解释，并提供基于上下文的预处理、多层次标注和模型基线结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有毒性检测数据集通常忽略上下文依赖性和隐式表达，且多集中于单一语言，难以捕捉跨文化和事件相关语境中的复杂毒性行为。为此，需要一个结合多语言、对话上下文和结构化推理模式的数据集来推动更精准、可解释的毒性识别研究。

Method: 构建了一个名为ToxiREX的数据集，收集来自与重大事件相关的Reddit帖子的多语言评论线程；采用先前研究中定义的毒性推理模式进行结构化标注；对训练集使用商用大语言模型自动标注，测试集由母语者人工标注；设计了适用于分层预测的评估策略，并通过提示和微调语言模型进行基线实验。

Result: 数据集包含六种语言（英语、阿拉伯语、土耳其语、西班牙语、德语和荷兰语），训练集12.5万条评论，测试集近3000条由母语者标注；发现测试集中的标注差异多为合理不同解释而非噪声；模型表现优于随机猜测但仍有显著提升空间，表明该任务具有挑战性。

Conclusion: ToxiREX是首个同时整合多语言、对话上下文和隐式毒性并采用结构化推理模式进行丰富标注的数据集，为毒性内容识别提供了更真实、细粒度的研究资源，展示了当前模型在理解复杂语境毒性方面的局限，推动未来更强大、可解释的检测方法发展。

Abstract: We introduce a new, contextual, multilingual dataset called ToxiREX: Toxic REasoning in ConteXt. The dataset consists of threads of Reddit comments and structured characterizations of what the comments imply, following a systematic toxic reasoning schema developed in a previous paper. Using the schema allows us to capture and explain implicit and context-dependent toxicity, while supporting mappings to existing toxicity taxonomies. The dataset includes comments in six languages (English, Arabic, Turkish, Spanish, German, and Dutch), collected from posts connected to specific major events (e.g. the 2023 Turkey earthquakes; the Russian invasion of Ukraine). We describe the context-preserving preprocessing of the threads. We create a training set of 125 thousand comments which is annotated by a commercially available LLM, and a test set of just under three thousand comments that is annotated by native speakers. We show that apparent disagreements in the test set annotations often reflect defensible alternative interpretations rather than noise. Finally, we provide baseline results by prompting and fine-tuning language models. To produce these results, we develop evaluation strategies for our hierarchical, schema-based predictions. While models perform better than random, there remains a lot of room for improvement, showing the task to be challenging. ToxiREX is the first dataset to simultaneously incorporate multiple languages, conversational context, and implicit toxicity, while using the toxic reasoning schema for rich, structured annotations. Dataset available at: https://github.com/cltl/toxirex

</details>


### [123] [Dialogue to Detection: A Multimodal Hybrid NLP Pipeline for Insurance Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28002)
*Muhammad Shakeel Akram,Amal Htait,Abdul Hamid Sadka,Emma Meisingseth,Karishma Jaitly*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种合成多模态框架，用于在首次损失通知（FNOL）阶段检测保险欺诈，结合文本、行为和说话者特征，提供可复现的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有保险欺诈检测方法主要依赖私有的纯文本数据集，限制了多模态方法的发展，且难以在FNOL阶段实现早期检测。

Method: 构建一个模拟FNOL场景的合成多模态框架，生成代理人与客户的对话转录和双说话者音频，进行ASR和说话人分离；下游模块结合命名实体识别、正则特征提取、基于LLM-RAG的信息检索和说话人嵌入，通过规则基础的风险评分系统检测叙述重复、结构不一致和跨案例语音重复。

Result: 数据集验证和组件级评估表明该框架具有稳定性与迁移潜力，能够在多模态条件下有效支持欺诈检测。

Conclusion: 该框架为保险欺诈检测提供了超越纯文本方法的可复现多模态基线，有助于推动早期欺诈识别研究与应用。

Abstract: Insurance fraud imposes substantial financial losses and operational inefficiencies, raising premiums and impacting trust among legitimate policyholders. Early detection at FNOL remains a persistent challenge. Existing approaches rely largely on private, text-only datasets, limiting progress on multimodal methods that integrate linguistic, behavioural, and speaker-based indicators. We introduce a synthetic multimodal framework that replicates FNOL conditions. It generates agent-customer dialogue transcripts and two-speaker audios, performs ASR and diarisation. Downstream modules combine NER, regex-based feature extraction, LLM-RAG retrieval, and speaker embeddings in a rule-based risk score to flag narrative reuse, structural inconsistencies, and cross-case voice repetition while balancing sensitivity and false positives. Dataset validation and component-level evaluations show stability and transfer potential, offering a reproducible baseline beyond text-only fraud detection.

</details>


### [124] [The Signal-Coverage Matrix: Stratifying Type and Semantic Errors in Statement Autoformalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28013)
*Chengxiao Dai,Zhaokun Yan,Zhanhui Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种信号-覆盖矩阵来更细致地分析大型语言模型自动形式化中的类型正确性提升，揭示了不同类型错误的修复情况，并指出应根据具体改进的错误类型而非单一指标来评估性能增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的类型正确性（TC%）指标过于简化，无法反映不同方法在解决特定错误类型上的实际效果，因此需要一种更细粒度的分析方法。

Method: 引入信号-覆盖矩阵，结合Lean elaborator的通过/失败判断与语义等价性判断，将输出分为四类：真正成功（TS）、仅类型成功（TO）、仅语义成功（SO）和两者都失败（BF），并在多个数据集和方法上进行实验分析。

Result: 实验发现三种elab-feedback方法的TS增益中约64%来自类型层次的恢复，而语义层次基本持平；TO到TS的转换率为23/61，且该比率能较好预测新方法的TS变化；评委间在elab-feedback输出上的分歧较大，部分源于elaborator强制重写导致的误判。

Conclusion: TC%的提升应依据其来源的具体错误类别进行归因，而非仅依赖总体百分比，以实现更准确的方法评估与比较。

Abstract: Headline type-correctness (TC\%) of LLM autoformalization has climbed from $\sim$53\% to $\sim$76\% in two years, yet this scalar conceals which errors each method resolves. We propose a signal-coverage matrix that crosses the Lean elaborator (pass/fail) with a semantic-equivalence judgment (equivalent/not), sorting every output into one of four cells: true success (TS), type-only (TO), semantic-only (SO), or both fail (BF). On ProofNet\# and MiniF2F-test with DeepSeek V4-Pro across Vanilla, Lean-Retry, Sample-Filter, and Stratified Autoformalization (SAF): (1) the +34 to +36 TS gain across the three elab-feedback methods is $\sim$64\% type-stratum recovery, with SO flat on net (87.5\% of original semantic errors rescued, 8 newly created). (2) The TO-to-TS rate is 23/61 for each method (Wilson 95\% CI [26.6\%, 50.3\%]), and this stratum-level recovery rate predicts $Δ$TS on held-out methods to within 2/186 and renders $Δ$TC linear in the Vanilla elab-fail rate across six (model, dataset) cells ($R^2=0.96$). (3) The two judges disagree by 26 to 37 pp on elab-feedback outputs (vs. 7 pp on Vanilla), with 30 to 56\% of symbolic-judge false negatives traceable to elaborator-forced rewrites. The persistent residual reduces to two gold-formalization errors. TC\% gains should be credited by which cell moved, not by the scalar alone.

</details>


### [125] [A Tree-of-Thoughts Inspired Hybrid Approach for Legal Case Judgement Summarization using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28044)
*Aniket Deroy,Kripabandhu Ghosh,Saptarshi Ghosh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受“思维树”启发的提取-抽象混合式法律判决摘要方法，并通过实验验证其优于传统的提取式和抽象式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的法律判决摘要研究主要集中在传统的提取式和抽象式方法，而对混合式方法探索不足，因此需要一种更有效的混合技术来提升摘要质量。

Method: 提出一种基于树状思维（tree-of-thoughts）的提取-抽象混合摘要方法，并使用DeepSeek和LLama两种主流大语言模型进行实验，比较不同类型提示在摘要任务中的表现。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的提取-抽象混合提示方法生成的摘要质量优于纯提取式和纯抽象式方法。

Conclusion: 基于树状思维的混合式摘要方法在法律判决文本上具有更好的性能，为法律领域的大语言模型应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: In recent times, Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being used for legal case judgement summarization. Most prior works have tried traditional extractive and abstractive summarization of case judgements. However, hybrid or extractive-abstractive techniques have not been explored much. In this work, we propose a novel tree-of-thoughts inspired extractive-abstractive summarization approach for legal judgement summarization. We conduct experiments using two popular LLMs, DeepSeek and LLama, and compare among extractive, abstractive and extractive-abstractive summarization. Our experiments show that the proposed extractive-abstractive prompt provides better summaries compared to other types of LLM prompts.

</details>


### [126] [Can LLMs Judge Better Than They Generate? Evaluating Task Asymmetry, Mechanistic Interpretability and Transferability for In-Context QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28050)
*Sambaran Bandyopadhyay*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，在特定的问答设置中，生成答案比自我评估更容易，挑战了现有自评估流程的核心假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验在控制条件下的上下文问答任务中，评估是否真的比生成更简单。

Method: 通过四个基准数据集（SQuAD 2.0, DROP, HotpotQA, MuSiQue）和两种模型进行实验，使用注意力分析和LoRA微调探究生成与评估的差异。

Result: 在四个基准中的三个上，生成准确率超过自我评估；注意力分析显示评估对上下文的关注度远低于生成，且几乎不关注候选答案；LoRA微调表明该不对称性并非训练产物。

Conclusion: 评估并不总是比生成更容易，这一发现挑战了LLM-as-a-Judge和自评估管道的基本假设。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge and self-evaluation pipelines implicitly assume that evaluation is easier than generation. We test this in a controlled in-context QA setting where a context passage is the sole information source and each model judges the answer it generated, removing the parametric-knowledge confound of open-domain comparisons. Across four benchmarks (SQuAD 2.0, DROP, HotpotQA, MuSiQue) and two models, evaluation is not uniformly easier: generation accuracy exceeds self-evaluation on three of four, with multi-hop MuSiQue the exception. Attention analysis reveals why: evaluation attends to context 3--5x less than generation does and barely reads the candidate answer. LoRA fine-tuning confirms the asymmetry is not a training artifact: generation fine-tuning induces over-acceptance and evaluation fine-tuning degrades generation. These findings challenge core assumptions in self-evaluation pipelines.

</details>


### [127] [MultiHashFormer: Hash-based Generative Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28057)
*Huiyin Xue,Atsuki Yamaguchi,Nikolaos Aletras*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MultiHashFormer是一种新的框架，通过多哈希函数生成唯一哈希签名来实现基于哈希的自回归，解决了传统哈希方法在因果语言模型中的限制，在多个参数规模下均优于标准Transformer模型，并支持多语言词汇扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决现有哈希方法因多对一冲突而无法用于因果语言模型的问题，同时保持参数效率。

Method: 提出MultiHashFormer框架：每个token由多个独立哈希函数生成的短离散哈希ID序列（即哈希签名）表示；使用Hash Encoder将签名压缩为单一潜在向量供Transformer解码器处理；Hash Decoder生成下一个token的哈希签名并映射回文本。

Result: 在100M、1B和3B参数规模上评估显示，MultiHashFormer在多个基准测试中持续优于标准Transformer语言模型，并能在不修改结构的情况下以恒定参数量支持多语言词汇扩展。

Conclusion: MultiHashFormer成功实现了哈希机制下的自回归建模，兼顾了参数效率与模型性能，具备良好的扩展性和多语言适应能力。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) represent tokens using embedding matrices that scale linearly with the vocabulary size. To constrain the parameter footprint, prior work proposes hashing many tokens into a single vector within encoder-only models. While this offers parameter efficiency, many-to-one collisions prevent its use in causal LMs. In this paper, we propose MultiHashFormer, a new framework that allows hash-based autoregression. Each token is represented as a unique hash signature, a short sequence of discrete hash IDs, generated by multiple independent hash functions. A Hash Encoder compresses this signature into a single latent vector for processing by a Transformer decoder. Then, a Hash Decoder generates the hash signature of the next token, which is then mapped back to text. We evaluate our approach at the 100M, 1B and 3B parameter scales, demonstrating that MultiHashFormer consistently outperforms standard Transformer LMs across multiple benchmarks. Furthermore, we show that our model handles multilingual vocabulary expansion with a constant parameter footprint without any modifications.

</details>


### [128] [Mechanism-Driven Monitors for Preemptive Detection of LLM Training Instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28116)
*Ruixuan Huang,Yipei Wang,Wenyi Fang,Hantao Huang,Yifan Huang,Ansheng You,Zhenxing Zhang,Shuai Wang,Fan Wu,Yang Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于模块功能角色的机制驱动方法，用于检测大语言模型训练中的不稳定性，能够在损失完全崩溃前数千步检测到故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于大规模语言模型训练消耗大量计算资源，训练过程中的不稳定性会导致高昂的成本，因此需要尽早检测训练故障。

Method: 从关键模块的功能角色出发，设计内部监控器，监测如低精度Flash Attention中的QK双线性分解的谱熵以及MoE路由器的专家选择行为。

Result: 在低精度注意力、大学习率及复合故障的故障注入实验中，这些信号能在损失发散前数千步检测到不同类型的故障。

Conclusion: 所提出的机制驱动监控方法能有效提前检测训练不稳定性，具有较强的可解释性和通用性。

Abstract: Frontier large language model training consumes massive accelerator fleets and long wall-clock computation, making stability failures costly when they occur. After a numerical or a hyperparameter fault has already destabilized the training dynamics, it may continue for thousands of steps while loss and gradient norms still appear normal. We study mechanism-driven detection of training instability by deriving internal monitors from the functional role of each critical module and from the earliest computational sites where failures are expected to produce measurable signatures. For low-precision flash attention, we monitor the spectral entropy of a QK bilinear decomposition, whose first-order term becomes abnormal before the loss fully collapses. For MoE routers, we derive indicators from their role in expert selection. Our fault-injection experiments on low-precision attention, large learning-rate, and combined faults show that these signals provide distinct signatures for different failures, triggering thousands of steps before loss divergence.

</details>


### [129] [From Tokens to States: LLMs as a Special Case of World Models and the Continuous Path Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28127)
*Paul Dubois*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了大语言模型（LLM）与世界模型之间的关系，提出LLM是世界模型的一种退化特例，并指出从NTP到JEPA存在一个自然的连续谱系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战当前将LLM与世界模型对立的观点，重新定义二者关系以推动通用人工智能的发展。

Method: 通过概念分析和理论推导，论证LLM作为世界模型特例的可能性，并梳理从token预测到潜在空间预测的连续研究谱系。

Result: 建立了LLM与世界模型之间的连续统一体框架，识别出数据和架构两个关键研究挑战。

Conclusion: LLM并非与世界模型对立，而是其特殊形式；迈向更强大模型需解决自监督数据获取和连续状态建模的新架构问题。

Abstract: The AI community has framed the relationship between large language models (LLMs) and world models as a dichotomy: LLMs predict tokens; world models simulate reality. Yann LeCun argues in 2022 that reaching general intelligence requires abandoning autoregressive token prediction in favour of latent-space architectures. This framing is unnecessarily binary. Two claims will be defended. First, LLMs are a degenerate special case of world models: the state space is the set of all token sequences, the only action is appending one token, and world models are therefore a strict generalisation of LLMs, not a replacement. Second, there is a natural continuous spectrum from NTP to JEPA, with multi-token prediction, future-summary prediction, and next-latent prediction as intermediate stations already populated by current research. Moving along this spectrum relaxes the LLM constraints one by one. It also progressively surrenders the two practical advantages that make LLMs trainable at scale: internet-scale self-supervised data, and a transformer architecture co-designed for discrete token prediction. Both are examined as open research questions: the data question (the cliff from self-supervised text to instrumented action-labelled environments) and the architecture question (whether the transformer generalises to continuous-state prediction, or whether a new primitive is needed).

</details>


### [130] [Cognitive Episodes in LLM Reasoning Traces Enable Interpretable Human Item Difficulty Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28186)
*Chenguang Wang,Ming Li,Xinyue Zeng,Zhuochun Li,Hong Jiao,Tianyi Zhou,Dawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Epi2Diff 是一个利用大推理模型（LRM）的推理轨迹来预测教育测评中题目难度的新框架，通过将推理过程分解为认知上有意义的片段序列，实现更准确且可解释的难度预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖昂贵的人类校准或仅基于题目文本表示，难以捕捉导致题目难度的认知过程。本文主张将难度视为问题解决负担的表现，并利用大推理模型提供的推理轨迹作为可扩展的过程证据。

Method: 提出 Epi2Diff 框架，将大推理模型的推理轨迹映射为基于认知的片段序列，提取推理规模、努力分配和状态转换等动态特征，并与题目语义表示结合用于人类难度预测。

Result: 在四个真实的人类难度数据集上，Epi2Diff 均优于强基线方法，在 SAT 分类任务上平均相对提升 8.1%；分析表明更难的题目引发更费力、迭代性更强、以实现为中心的推理模式。

Conclusion: 大推理模型推理轨迹中的认知片段可作为预测且可解释的人类题目难度的过程表征，为基于推理模型的教育测量提供了新视角。

Abstract: Predicting human item difficulty is central to educational assessment, where reliable estimates support fairness and effective test construction. Existing methods often depend on costly human calibration or item-level textual representations, providing limited evidence about the cognitive processes that make items difficult. We argue that difficulty should be viewed not only as a property of item text, but also as an observable consequence of the problem-solving burden an item induces. Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) offer scalable process evidence through reasoning traces, but such evidence must be structured to support interpretable modeling. To this end, we introduce Epi2Diff (Episode to Difficulty), a framework that maps LRM reasoning traces into cognitively grounded episode sequences. These episodes group trace segments into functional problem-solving states, enabling difficulty to be modeled through reasoning scale, effort allocation, and state transitions. Epi2Diff extracts compact episode-dynamic features and combines them with semantic item representations for human difficulty prediction. Experiments on four real-world human difficulty datasets show that Epi2Diff consistently outperforms strong baselines, including fine-tuned small language models, LLM in-context learning, and supervised LLM adaptation. On SAT-derived classification benchmarks, Epi2Diff achieves an 8.1% average relative gain over supervised LLM fine-tuning baselines. Further analyses show that harder items induce more effortful, iterative, and implementation-centered episode dynamics, rather than merely longer responses. These results demonstrate that cognitive episodes in LRM reasoning traces provide a predictive and interpretable process representation for human item difficulty, offering a new lens for educational measurement with reasoning models.

</details>


### [131] [Vision-Default, Prior-Override: Causal Mechanisms of Perception-Knowledge Conflict in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28273)
*Niclas Lietzow,Danielle Bitterman,Carsten Eickhoff,William Rudman,Michal Golovanevsky*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过激活补丁和消融分析，揭示了视觉-语言模型中感知与知识冲突的稀疏因果机制，发现少数注意力头在知识引导回答中起关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解视觉-语言模型如何在视觉证据与先验知识冲突时进行决策，并提供组件级的因果解释。

Method: 结合残差流、注意力头和MLP子层三个粒度的激活补丁与模型组件消融研究，并进行机制分析。

Result: 发现视觉 grounding 默认出现，而先验知识 grounding 依赖于网络后半部分少量关键注意力头（2.5%-4.8%），这些头在消融后使68%-96%的知识引导预测转为视觉引导，但仅影响0.8%-7.5%的视觉引导预测；这些头可分为路由头和写入头。

Conclusion: 视觉-语言模型中存在跨模型家族和规模一致的稀疏因果回路，用于处理感知与知识的冲突。

Abstract: Vision-language models must reconcile visual evidence with memorized world knowledge when the two conflict. How they resolve this conflict shapes the reliability of multimodal systems, yet prior work characterizes it behaviorally without a component-level causal account. We combine activation patching across three granularities (residual stream, attention heads, and MLP sublayers) with model-component ablation studies and mechanistic analysis. Across three VLM families, we find that visual grounding emerges by default, whereas prior grounding depends on a small set of causally necessary attention heads (2.5-4.8%) concentrated in the second half of the network. These heads enable answers from stored world knowledge (e.g., "red" for a strawberry) despite conflicting visual input. Ablating them flips predictions from knowledge-grounded to visually grounded answers in 68-96% of cases under prior-knowledge prompts, but changes only 0.8-7.5% of visually grounded predictions, establishing an asymmetric causal structure. The identified heads decompose into routing heads, which modulate information flow, and writing heads, which directly project answer tokens into the residual stream. This structure is consistent across model families and scales, revealing a sparse causal circuit underlying perception-knowledge conflict in VLMs.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [Support-Constrained RL Enables Real-World Policy Improvement without Real-World Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27475)
*Raymond Yu,William Huey,Mustafa Mukadam,Anusha Nagabandi,Abhishek Gupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SCORE的实到仿真再到实的强化学习框架，通过在仿真中约束策略优化的行为支持域，实现了真实世界机器人操作策略的高效且安全的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于真实数据训练的机器人策略存在不精确、缓慢和对扰动敏感的问题，而直接在仿真中进行无约束强化学习会因动力学差异导致策略无法迁移。因此需要一种既能利用仿真效率又能保证策略可转移性的方法。

Method: 提出Support-Constrained Off-Domain REinforcement (SCORE)框架，利用预训练于真实数据上的生成性基础策略定义行为的支持域，并通过流引导(flow steering)机制限制仿真中的强化学习只生成基础策略能产生的动作，从而避免接触与动力学不匹配带来的问题。

Result: 在八个真实世界的灵巧多指操作任务上，SCORE将平均成功率从37.8%提升至89.9%，优于最佳基线的59.5%，同时达到成功所需的步数减少了36.8%。

Conclusion: 适当约束下的仿真环境可以显著提升真实世界机器人操作策略的性能，SCORE提供了一种新的实-仿-实策略改进范式。

Abstract: Robots trained on real world data tend to be imprecise, slow, and brittle to perturbations. Improving these policies with reinforcement learning (RL) is an appealing alternative, but this process often requires expensive training in the real world. Performing policy improvement in simulation instead provides a far cheaper alternative, but unconstrained RL in simulation can exploit contact and dynamics mismatches, resulting in unsafe behaviors that do not transfer to hardware. Common forms of regularization can furthermore limit improvement by overconstraining to an imperfect behavior prior. In this work, we propose Support-Constrained Off-Domain REinforcement (SCORE), a real-to-sim-to-real framework that constrains RL in simulation to the support of a generative policy pretrained on real data. We instantiate this constraint through flow steering, restricting SCORE to actions the base policy can already produce, which ensures transferable behaviors while maximizing policy improvement. Improving a policy with SCORE requires minimal effort: it learns from sparse rewards, avoids distillation, and leaves the base policy untouched. Across eight real-world dexterous multi-fingered robotic manipulation tasks, SCORE improves average success rate from 37.8% to 89.9%, compared to 59.5% for the best baseline, and reaches success in 36.8% fewer steps than the base policy. Ultimately, through extensive experiments and ablations, we show that simulation can substantially improve real-world manipulation policies when policy optimization is appropriately constrained, introducing a new paradigm for real-to-sim-to-real policy improvement. Videos and code are available at https://weirdlabuw.github.io/score/.

</details>


### [133] [AO-ARC: Almost-Surely Asymptotically Optimal Multi-Robot Motion Planning with ARC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27495)
*James D. Motes,Marco Morales,Nancy M. Amato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为AO-ARC的任意时间多机器人运动规划方法，能够在初始求解速度上与当前最优的可行性求解器相当，并在机器人数量增加时比现有任意时间方法更快、更可靠地收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在多机器人系统中实现快速初始解并逐步优化路径，同时应对随着机器人数量增加带来的可扩展性和收敛可靠性问题。

Method: 采用AO-x元算法框架，通过在有界MRMP实例上迭代调用ARC方法，并基于最大完工时间（makespan）代价度量，结合自适应耦合/解耦机制来实现 anytime 优化。

Result: 理论分析证明了AO-ARC具有渐近最优性；实验在不同协调复杂度的2D场景和一个3D机械臂场景中验证了其性能，结果表明其收敛更快且更可靠。

Conclusion: AO-ARC在保持初始解快速生成的同时，展现出优越的渐近最优性和可扩展性，适用于实际多机器人应用。

Abstract: We present AO-ARC, an anytime multi-robot motion planning (MRMP) method that achieves initial solution times on par with state-of-the-art MRMP feasibility solvers while converging faster and more reliably than existing anytime MRMP methods as the number of robots increases. AO-ARC adapts the AO-x meta-algorithm for converting feasibility solvers into anytime algorithms by iteratively calling the original ARC method on bounded MRMP instances under a makespan cost metric. This exploits the adaptive (de)coupling of ARC while maintaining the consistent cost bound across robot (de)compositions needed for AO-x. We provide theoretical analysis proving the asymptotic optimality properties of AO- ARC and conduct empirical evaluation on a set of 2D scenarios with different levels of coordination complexity and a 3D manipulator scenario representative of real-world applications.

</details>


### [134] [Spacecraft Fiducial Marker for Autonomous Rendezvous, Proximity Operations, and Docking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27566)
*Ravi Kumar Thakur,Matouš Vrba,Martin Saska*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种专为空间机器人操作设计的多尺度视觉标记AstraTag，基于自相似的Spidron图案，能够在不同距离和曲面上实现稳定检测，相比现有方法在弯曲表面具有更高的检测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉标记多为单尺度且针对地面机器人设计，在近距离操作时易丢失视野，难以满足空间机器人在交会对接等关键阶段的高可靠性需求。

Method: 提出AstraTag标记，采用递归自相似的方形Spidron模板，利用48位签名和广义里德-所罗门码进行标识；检测流程包括基于轮廓的四边形定位、透视归一化和字典匹配，并引入薄板样条（TPS）重映射以处理曲面变形。

Result: 在平面和曲面航天器模型上与Fractal ArUco和AprilTag进行对比测试，AstraTag在曲面上表现出更高的检测成功率。

Conclusion: AstraTag通过其多尺度结构和针对曲面优化的重映射机制，为空间机器人在轨操作提供了一种鲁棒的视觉标记解决方案。

Abstract: Robotic operations in space are challenging due to the harsh environment and the high cost of failure. Fiducial markers provide visual references that aid autonomous rendezvous, proximity operations, and docking for space robots. However, existing fiducial markers are mostly single-scale and largely designed for terrestrial robotics. Such markers leave the camera's field of view at close range, precisely during the proximity and docking phases where reliable tracking is most critical. This paper presents AstraTag, a fiducial marker designed for autonomous on-orbit robotic operations. The marker template is based on a square Spidron pattern whose recursive, self-similar structure enables detection across multiple spatial scales. Marker identification uses a 48-bit signature derived from triangular sub-regions of the template and encoded with a Generalised Reed-Solomon (GRS) code. The detection pipeline performs contour-based quadrilateral localisation, perspective normalisation, and signature matching against a pre-computed dictionary. To handle markers affixed to curved spacecraft surfaces, it incorporates a Thin-Plate Spline (TPS) re-warp fallback that exploits the marker's internal rectangular borders as additional geometric correspondences. We benchmark AstraTag against three-layer Fractal ArUco and AprilTag on spacecraft mockups with flat and curved surfaces. On curved surfaces, AstraTag achieves a higher detection rate than both baselines, offering a robust recursive-marker option for space robotics.

</details>


### [135] [SceneBot: Contact-Prompted General Humanoid Whole Body Tracking with Scene-Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27581)
*Sirui Chen,Shibo Zhao,Zhen Wu,Jiaman Li,Guanya Shi,C. Karen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SceneBot是一个统一的运动跟踪框架，通过结合参考动作和每链接触标签，成功实现了自由空间运动、地形穿越和全身操作的无缝整合，首次在复杂长周期任务中展示了接触条件作为人形控制强大接口的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的人形强化学习策略在自由空间运动中表现出色，但在涉及丰富接触的任务（如与物体和不平坦地形交互）中表现不佳，因为纯运动学跟踪无法解决物理交互中的模糊性。

Method: 提出SceneBot框架，利用参考动作和每链接触标签来明确环境交互；并通过回溯场景重建方法从重定向的人类动作中推断场景交互图，以生成缺少的标注交互数据。

Result: 在7.5小时重建的接触丰富数据上训练后，SceneBot能够泛化到未见过的动作和环境中，成功执行如搬箱子上楼等复杂任务。

Conclusion: SceneBot是首个能够无缝整合自由空间与接触丰富行为的通用框架，验证了接触条件作为人形控制接口的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: Current humanoid reinforcement-learning policies excel at free-space motions but struggle with contact-rich tasks, as pure kinematic tracking cannot resolve the physical ambiguities of interacting with objects and uneven terrain. To address this, we introduce SceneBot, a unified motion-tracking framework capable of handling freespace locomotion, terrain traversal, and whole-body manipulation. SceneBot conditions a single policy on both reference motions and per-link contact labels, explicitly defining expected environmental interactions. To overcome the lack of annotated interaction data, we propose a hindsight scene reconstruction approach that infers scene-interaction graphs from retargeted human motion. Trained on 7.5 hours of this reconstructed, contact-rich data, SceneBot successfully generalizes to unseen motions and environments. Our results demonstrate that SceneBot is the first general framework to seamlessly unify free-space and contact-rich behaviors executing complex, long-horizon tasks like carrying a box upstairs and establishing contact conditioning as a powerful interface for humanoid control. All code and data will be open-sourced. More demos and information are available at: https://ericcsr.github.io/scenebot/

</details>


### [136] [Learning to Throw: Agile and Accurate Cable-Suspended Payload Delivery with a Quadrotor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27603)
*Yifan Zhai,Elia Raimondi,Yunfan Ren,Ismail Geles,Yannick Armati,Jiaxu Xing,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合高保真四旋翼模型与物理引擎的混合仿真框架，利用深度强化学习实现四旋翼无人机对悬挂负载的敏捷精准投掷，显著优于传统基于模型的方法，并开源了仿真器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于模型的轨迹优化方法在四旋翼携带负载投掷任务中受限于保守约束、跟踪误差和难以精确建模绳索动力学，导致性能不佳，尤其在高动态精准投掷场景下表现不足。

Method: 提出一种混合仿真框架，将高保真四旋翼解析模型与物理引擎耦合，通过每一步交换力实现对绳索-负载系统的高精度模拟；在此基础上使用深度强化学习训练控制策略，实现快速准确的目标投掷，并验证了零样本迁移到真实硬件的有效性。

Result: 所提RL策略在真实设备上零样本部署即减少着陆误差最多50%，投掷时间减少最多30%，显著优于基于模型的基线方法；消融实验表明耦合仿真是性能提升的关键；同时验证了基于视觉观测的策略可达到与状态输入相当的精度。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，结合高保真模拟与深度强化学习可有效解决复杂绳索-负载动力学下的高动态飞行投掷问题，推动了空中灵巧操作的发展，并通过开源仿真器促进后续研究。

Abstract: Quadrotors offer the agility needed to rapidly transport suspended payloads during time-critical applications, including search-and-rescue and medical delivery. While suspended-payload transport and traversal for these missions are well studied, the highly dynamic targeted release of the payload remains comparatively underexplored. State-of-the-art approaches typically rely on model-based trajectory optimization and tracking; however, these methods often yield sub-optimal performance due to conservative feasibility constraints, tracking errors, and the inherent difficulty of analytically modeling flexible rope dynamics. To overcome these limitations, we propose a hybrid simulation framework that couples a high-fidelity analytical quadrotor model with a physics solver for complex rope and payload interactions. By exchanging forces between the two domains at every step, we obtain a physically accurate simulation of the suspended-payload system. Leveraging this environment, we train a deep reinforcement learning (RL) policy that executes agile, accurate payload throws to designated targets. Deployed zero-shot on hardware, our RL policy pushes the boundary of the agility-accuracy trade-off, outperforming the model-based baseline by reducing the landing error by up to 50% and the throw duration by up to 30%. Ablation studies confirm that the coupled simulation is the key enabler of these gains. We further show that the same pipeline trains a policy driven by visual observations rather than an explicit state estimate, achieving accuracy comparable to that of the state-based policy. To accelerate future research in dynamic aerial manipulation, we open-source the simulator to the community upon acceptance.

</details>


### [137] [Physics-Guided Robotic Radiation Source Localization along Arbitrary Measurement Paths in Unstructured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27624)
*Hojoon Son,Kai Tan,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于物理信息机器学习的机器人辐射源定位框架，能够在未知环境中不依赖特定路径精确估计辐射源位置，并通过仿真和物理实验验证了其鲁棒性和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在辐射源定位时需让机器人靠近源，增加了辐射损伤风险，且路径规划灵活性差，限制了机器人在放射性环境中的多任务能力。

Method: 设计了物理启发的模型张量用于处理未知障碍物下的伽马射线衰减信号，采用并行计算多个物理信息机器学习模型，并结合连续学习技术实现在线定位。

Result: 在高保真模拟环境中通过蒙特卡洛粒子输运方法验证了方法的有效性，适用于多种空间尺度、辐射源类型、障碍物材质与几何形状及机器人轨迹，并通过未见配置的物理实验验证了泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了从点状通量检测到空间智能感知的跨越，提升了机器人在复杂放射性环境中的自主定位能力与任务灵活性。

Abstract: Using robots to estimate the location of the radiation source is an effective way to improve efficiency and safety. Existing methods focus on planning the robot's path to achieve precise estimation, typically approaching the source. However, approaching the source increases the risk of radiation damage to a robot. In addition, a path-planning algorithm designed solely for radiation source localization (RSL) limits the flexibility of missions that deploy robots into radioactive environments. This study presents an automation framework for robotic RSL that leverages a physics-informed machine learning (PIML) model to precisely estimate the source location, regardless of measurement paths, in unknown environments. Physics-inspired model tensors have been designed for PIML to handle attenuated gamma-ray flux signals from unknown obstacles, and multiple models are computed in parallel to improve the robustness and precision of the RSL. The proposed method is evaluated in high-fidelity simulation environments using Monte Carlo particle transport across diverse randomized domains, including spatial scales, radiation source types, obstacle materials and geometries, and robot trajectories. The method is also validated through physical experiments on configurations that are not included in the simulation-based evaluation. The continuous learning technique is applied in real-robot deployment to enhance the practical applicability of the online robotic RSL system. The proposed method advances robot radiation perception from pointwise flux detection to spatial intelligence.

</details>


### [138] [P-ARC: Exploiting Subproblem Independence for Parallel Multi-Robot Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27625)
*James D. Motes,Marco Morales,Nancy M. Amato*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了P-ARC，即自适应机器人协调（ARC）的并行变体，用于多机器人运动规划（MRMP），并通过OR-并行多起点策略进一步优化，形成混合并行策略OR-P-ARC。实验表明，在使用16个CPU核心时，大型Panda多机械臂团队的规划时间加速接近4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高多机器人运动规划的效率，解决传统ARC方法在大规模场景下的计算瓶颈问题。

Method: 提出P-ARC，对ARC的三个主要阶段（初始个体解、冲突检测和冲突解决）进行并行化，并引入OR-并行多起点策略，形成OR-P-ARC。在不同规模的2D移动机器人和平面机械臂场景中评估性能。

Result: 在最多控制128个机器人的场景中测试了不同并行策略的影响；在真实场景启发的大型Panda多机械臂任务中，使用16核CPU实现了接近4倍的规划时间加速。

Conclusion: P-ARC及其混合并行策略OR-P-ARC显著提升了多机器人运动规划的效率，尤其在大规模复杂场景下具有良好的可扩展性和加速性能。

Abstract: This paper presents Parallel ARC (P-ARC), a parallel variant of the Adaptive Robot Coordination (ARC) approach to multi-robot motion planning (MRMP). P-ARC proposes a parallel variant for each of the three main stages in ARC: initial individual solutions, conflict detection, and conflict resolution, exploiting the independence created by ARC's decomposition of the MRMP problem. Additionally, we employ an OR-parallel multi-start strategy to both ARC and P-ARC, creating a hybrid parallel strategy OR-P-ARC. We evaluate the impact of the different parallel strategies for ARC using a set of scaling 2D mobile and planar manipulator scenarios with up to 128 robots to control for conflicts and work distribution across the stages of ARC. Additionally, we demonstrate planning time speedups approaching 4X over the sequential version for large Panda multi-manipulator teams in real-world inspired scenarios when deploying 16 CPU cores.

</details>


### [139] [Direct Action-Head Injection of A Grounded 3D Point Unlocks Spatial and Task Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27663)
*Shiang-Feng Tsai,Jin-Cheng Jhang,Yen-Ling Tai,Jia-Hong Lai,Shih-Yun Wong,KangTung-Hsu,Yi-Ting Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级、模型无关的模块，通过将3D点表示的定位信号直接注入动作头，显著提升了视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在空间和任务泛化上的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型在测试时对物体位置和任务指令的变化较为脆弱，现有方法未能有效解决这一问题。

Method: 设计了一个两层MLP模块，将3D点表示的定位信号与其相对于夹爪的位移结合，并通过自适应层归一化直接注入到动作头，无需修改VLA主干或预训练流程。

Result: 在LIBERO-PRO数据集上，GR00T-N1.6的平均成功率在任务扰动下从31.2提升至77.5，在位置扰动下从28.1提升至60.2；$π_{0.5}$模型也表现出类似增益，验证了方法的通用性。

Conclusion: 将充分的3D定位信号直接注入动作头是实现VLA模型空间与任务泛化的关键，仅需在预训练主干上添加轻量模块即可实现显著性能提升。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models leverage large-scale vision-language pretraining for flexible robot manipulation, yet at test time they remain brittle along two axes: spatial generalization, when object positions differ from those seen during training, and task generalization, when a familiar scene is paired with a different language instruction than the one seen in training. A growing family of methods addresses this brittleness by endowing a policy with the spatial and task-aware information such as 2D pixel-coordinate for object localization and placement. However, we find that existing representation through language prompting or visual prompting does not address the limitations; in contrast, exploiting a 3D point-based representation and feeding it directly to the action head leads to substantial improvements-revealing that how the grounding signal is represented and injected into the VLA is the true game changer. Thus, we propose a lightweight, model-agnostic module that represents the grounding signal in 3D, computes its relative displacement to the gripper, and injects the resulting spatial embedding directly into the action head through adaptive layer normalization. The entire module is a two-layer MLP that requires no changes to the VLA backbone or pretraining pipeline. On LIBERO-PRO, our method improves the average success rate of GR00T-N1.6 from 31.2 to 77.5 points under task perturbation and from 28.1 to 60.2 points under position perturbation (gains of 46.3 and 32.1 points). Comparable gains are achieved for $π_{0.5}$ as well, demonstrating that the mechanism is backbone-agnostic. Together, these results support our central finding: given adequate grounding lifted into 3D, injecting it directly into the action head is what unlocks both spatial and task generalization in VLAs-achievable with nothing more than a lightweight module on top of a pretrained backbone.

</details>


### [140] [CWI: Composite Humanoid Whole-Body Imitation System for Loco-manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27676)
*Wenqi Ge,Junde Guo,Zhen Fu,Shunpeng Yang,Jiayu Chen,Hua Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Composite Whole-Body Imitation (CWI) 的框架，通过解耦上身操作与下身运动的动捕数据使用，实现了稳定且协调的人形机器人全身控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全身控制器在仅依赖命令采样或完整动捕数据时，分别面临稀疏奖励、动作不自然或数据不平衡等问题，难以实现自然且稳定的全身协调控制。

Method: CWI框架将上身操作与下身运动分开处理：利用完整的动捕数据训练上身操作，同时通过两个对抗运动先验（AMP）判别器引导稳定、指令条件下的下身运动；采用多批评家架构缓解目标冲突，并通过教师-学生蒸馏得到仅依赖双手姿态和速度/高度指令的全身策略。

Result: 在仿真和真实全尺寸LimX Oli人形机器人上的实验表明，该方法实现了具有竞争力的运动-操作性能、鲁棒的全身协调能力，并支持无需全身动捕设备的实际遥操作。

Conclusion: CWI通过解耦上下身控制与对抗式运动先验，有效结合了丰富操作数据与高质量步态控制，提升了人形机器人在复杂任务中的实用性与协调性。

Abstract: Achieving everyday tasks with humanoid robots requires coordinating stable locomotion with versatile manipulation. However, existing whole-body controllers still face significant challenges. Methods trained solely via command sampling, without motion-capture (MoCap) data, often struggle with sparse rewards and require carefully tuned curricula to converge. This is especially problematic for upper-body control, where the resulting motions deviate from human-like statistics and degrade whole-body coordination. Conversely, approaches that imitate full-body MoCap data suffer from dataset imbalance, as many locomotion trajectories are overly aggressive for stable-locomotion scenarios, necessitating extensive data filtering and augmentation. To address this, we present Composite Whole-Body Imitation (CWI), a framework that decouples the use of MoCap data for upper-body manipulation and lower-body locomotion. This decoupling allows us to exploit the full MoCap dataset of diverse manipulation references, while stable, command-conditioned lower-body locomotion is guided by dual discriminators trained on curated expert-quality walking and squatting clips via an Adversarial Motion Prior (AMP). A multi-critic architecture reduces conflicts among locomotion, manipulation, and motion-style objectives, and a teacher--student distillation stage yields a whole-body policy conditioned only on bimanual hand poses and velocity/height commands. We evaluate CWI through simulation experiments and real-world deployment on a full-size LimX Oli humanoid. The results show competitive loco-manipulation performance, robust whole-body coordination, and practical teleoperation without full-body motion-capture equipment. A project page with supplementary material can be found at https://cwi-ral.github.io/CWI-RAL-Webpage.

</details>


### [141] [DIM-WAM: World-Action Modeling with Diverse Historical Event Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27677)
*Kai Wang,Zhaopeng Gu,Yixiang Chen,Yuan Xu,Qisen Ma,Peng Su,Zhaowen Li,Yan Huang,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DiM-WAM是一种增强记忆的世界-动作模型，通过整合多尺度历史上下文、局部未来动态和全局任务进度，显著提升了机器人在长时程操作任务中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型依赖短期历史和短视界预测，难以应对依赖早期观测和全局任务进展的长时程、时序依赖性任务，存在长期遗忘和任务状态感知不足的问题。

Method: 提出DiM-WAM模型，引入多个独立更新的记忆库，通过相似性合并压缩存储视觉事件信息，并结合银行标识与时间嵌入读取长期上下文，用于视频与动作去噪；同时设计进度监督目标，促使记忆令牌编码历史事件及当前任务阶段与未来任务需求。

Result: 在RMBench上，任务成功率从LingBot-VA的28.4%提升至69.8%，优于Mem-0的42.0%；在四个真实Franka任务中，阶段成功率达91.5%（+20.8%），完整任务成功率达80.0%（+27.5%）。

Conclusion: DiM-WAM通过显式记忆机制和多尺度上下文建模，有效解决了长时程机器人操作中的长期依赖与任务进度感知问题，显著提升了模型性能。

Abstract: World-action models have shown promising robot-manipulation performance by jointly predicting future visual states and actions. However, existing methods mainly rely on short-term history and short-horizon future prediction, which is insufficient for long-horizon tasks whose correct execution depends on earlier observations and task progress. Such temporally dependent tasks require effective use of complementary temporal information, including recent local context, cross-stage historical events, immediate future dynamics, and global task progress. To address long-term forgetting and poor awareness of the global task state, we introduce DiM-WAM, a memory-augmented world-action model that integrates multi-scale historical context, local future dynamics, and global task progress. The memory extracts compact visual event information from real observations, updates multiple memory banks through independent similarity-based merging, and then reads the bank-identity- and time-embedded long-term context to condition video and action denoising. A progress-supervision objective further encourages memory tokens to encode not only completed historical events but also the current task stage and its implications for the remaining task. On RMBench, DiM-WAM raises average success from 28.4% with LingBot-VA to 69.8%, exceeding the explicit-memory Mem-0 baseline at 42.0%. On four real-world Franka tasks, it improves average stage success from 70.7% to 91.5% and full-task success from 52.5% to 80.0%. Project page: https://wangkai-casia.github.io/dim-wam/{\texttt{https://wangkai-casia.github.io/dim-wam/}}.

</details>


### [142] [Drop-Then-Recovery: How Redundant Are Vision-Language-Action Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27755)
*Guoheng Sun,Kaixi Feng,Shwai He,Xiaochuan Gong,Yexiao He,Ziyao Wang,Zheyu Shen,Wanghao Ye,Ramana Rao Kompella,Gaowen Liu,Ang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在机器人操控中的架构冗余问题，提出Drop-Then-Recovery（DTR）分析方法和GateProbe敏感性度量，发现语言主干高度冗余，而视觉和动作路径对移除更敏感，建议未来VLA架构应更合理分配各组件容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型继承自大型预训练VLM的语言主干，其容量远超短指令需求，存在潜在冗余，需探究哪些部分对闭环控制真正必要。

Method: 提出DTR分析协议：移除预训练VLA模型中的Transformer模块后微调，评估性能恢复情况；并设计GateProbe单次虚拟门控敏感性指标，用于排序模块对动作损失的贡献。

Result: 在多个VLA架构、操作基准及真实机器人场景中验证，发现语言主干高度冗余（如移除一半LLM模块反而使OpenVLA-OFT在LIBERO上从95.0%提升至98.3%），仅保留两个语言模块仍可恢复基线性能；而视觉与动作路径对移除更敏感。

Conclusion: 现有VLA基准任务对深层语言理解的压力有限，当前模型存在显著架构冗余，未来应更精细地分配语言、视觉和动作组件的容量。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models enable instruction-driven robotic manipulation, but they inherit oversized language backbones from pretrained VLMs whose capacity far exceeds what is needed for short robotic instructions. This raises a basic question: how much of a VLA model is actually necessary for closed-loop control? In this work, we study architectural redundancy in VLA models by using transformer block removal as a controlled intervention. We introduce \textbf{Drop-Then-Recovery (DTR)}, an analysis protocol that removes selected blocks from a pretrained VLA model and then fine-tunes the resulting model to measure whether the removed capacity was necessary for downstream control. To make this intervention reliable, we propose \textbf{GateProbe}, a one-shot virtual-gate sensitivity metric that ranks blocks by their contribution to the downstream action loss. Across multiple VLA architectures, manipulation benchmarks and even real-robot industrial scenarios, we find a strong asymmetry in post-removal recoverability: \ul{\textit{language backbones are highly redundant for standard robotic manipulation tasks, whereas vision and action pathways are substantially less tolerant to removal}}. On LIBERO, removing half of the LLM blocks even improves OpenVLA-OFT from 95.0% to 98.3% under the same downstream fine-tuning budget, and retaining only two language blocks still recovers baseline-level performance. These results suggest that current VLA benchmarks may exert limited pressure on deep language grounding and compositional instruction understanding, and that future VLA architectures should allocate capacity more deliberately across language, vision, and action components. The code is available at https://github.com/s1ghhh/VLADrop.

</details>


### [143] [SpikeVLA: Vision-Language-Action Models with Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27807)
*Ruiqi Song,Dujun Nie,Siyu Teng,Baiyong Ding,Xiaotong Zhang,Dong Li,Chenming Zhang,Yuchen Li,Hangbin Wu,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SpikeVLA是一种基于脉冲神经网络的视觉-语言-动作模型，通过事件驱动的稀疏计算显著降低能耗，适用于低功耗、实时具身智能任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大型Transformer的视觉-语言-动作模型存在高推理延迟和高能耗问题，限制了其在低功耗实时场景中的部署应用。

Method: 提出SpikeVLA架构，包含三个组件：脉冲视觉编码器Spike-V、多模态脉冲大语言模型Spike-L和脉冲动作策略网络Spike-A，均采用事件驱动的脉冲神经网络实现能量高效推理。

Result: 在导航与机器人控制任务中，SpikeVLA大幅降低了能耗与计算开销，同时保持具有竞争力的性能表现。

Conclusion: SpikeVLA为低功耗、实时具身智能提供了可行且高效的解决方案，展示了脉冲神经网络在复杂多模态任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have become a dominant paradigm for embodied intelligence. However, most existing approaches are built on large-scale transformers, resulting in substantial inference latency and energy consumption that limit their practical deployment in low-power, real-time scenarios. We propose SpikeVLA, a spiking VLA architecture for embodied navigation with energy-efficient inference, consisting of three key components. (i) A spiking vision encoder, Spike-V, that replaces dense continuous layers with event-driven spiking layers to reduce the energy consumption of visual representation learning. (ii) A multi-modal spiking large language model, Spike-L, that reformulates cross-modal reasoning with spiking dynamics and token-level event-driven sparsity to further lower computational cost. (iii) A spiking action policy network, Spike-A employs Laplacian-kernel population coding with a multi-layer fully connected SNN, and decodes spiking activities into stable and robust continuous control with energy-efficient inference under low-power constraints. Experiments on navigation and robotic control tasks show that SpikeVLA significantly reduces energy consumption and computational cost while maintaining competitive performance, highlighting its potential for low-power, real-time embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [144] [LXD-SLAM: LiDAR+X Dense SLAM with $\sum_{i=0}^{5}C_5^i$ Configurable Sensor Combinations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27811)
*Zhong Wang,Lin Zhang,Linfei Li,Ying Shen,Shaoming Zhang,Pengcheng Shi,Shengjie Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LXD-SLAM是一个高度通用的多传感器融合SLAM框架，以3D LiDAR为核心，支持LiDAR、相机、IMU、轮速计和GNSS等多种传感器即插即用组合，采用统一的迭代误差状态卡尔曼滤波器与高斯过程子网格地图表示，实现高精度、全局一致的实时稠密建图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SLAM系统在复杂环境中面临几何退化和传感器漂移问题，且多传感器融合方案常缺乏模块化设计、数学一致性不足或地图表达不优，难以适应多样化平台需求。

Method: 提出LXD-SLAM框架，基于3D LiDAR，支持最多32种传感器组合的即插即用；采用统一的迭代误差状态卡尔曼滤波器，结合自适应分层预测和点到网格距离最小化的更新策略；环境通过连续多层高斯过程（GP）子网格建模，支持高效的视觉特征深度恢复；引入基于GP子网格的扩展扫描上下文（ESC）描述子与双向PnP优化，实现在混合位姿图中的多模态闭环检测。

Result: 在公开数据集和真实场景中广泛实验表明，LXD-SLAM在多种配置下性能达到或超过当前最先进的专用里程计方法，可实时生成高保真、全局一致的稠密网格地图。

Conclusion: LXD-SLAM通过数学统一的滤波框架和先进的地图表示，实现了高模块化、强适应性的多传感器融合SLAM系统，适用于复杂环境下的自主导航，并为未来多传感器配置提供了通用解决方案。

Abstract: Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) is essential for autonomous systems, yet achieving reliable, globally consistent pose estimation and dense mapping in complex environments remains challenging due to geometric degeneracy and sensor drift. While multi-sensor fusion addresses these issues, existing systems often lack the modularity to adapt to diverse platforms and rely on mathematically inconsistent fusion or suboptimal map representations. To address these limitations, we propose LXD-SLAM (LiDAR+X Dense SLAM), a highly versatile and unified multi-sensor fusion framework. Centered around 3D LiDAR, our system allows for the plug-and-play integration of LiDAR, Camera, IMU, Wheel Encoder, and GNSS, supporting up to 32 distinct sensor combinations. We employ a mathematically unified Iterative Error-Sate Kalman Filter with an adaptive hierarchical prediction strategy and an update step that minimizes point-to-mesh distances and visual reprojection errors. To support this, the environment is modeled using continuous multi-layered Gaussian Process (GP) sub-meshes, which enables efficient ray-to-mesh depth recovery for visual features. For global consistency, we introduce an Extended Scan Context (ESC) descriptor derived from the GP sub-meshes alongside a Bidirectional PnP optimization for robust multi-modal loop closure within a hybrid pose graph. Extensive evaluations on public datasets and real-world experiments demonstrate that LXD-SLAM matches or exceeds state-of-the-art specialized odometry solutions across various configurations while generating high-fidelity, globally consistent dense meshes in real-time. The relevant codes and data will be made available at https://github.com/peterWon/LXD-SLAM upon publication.

</details>


### [145] [Booster Lab: A Data-Centric Pipeline for Learning Deployable Humanoid Locomotion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27813)
*Penghui Chen,Tinglong Zheng,Yufeng Zhang,Mingguo Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种以数据为中心的训练和部署流程，用于解决人形机器人运动学习中数据稀缺和物理可行性的问题，通过整合运动数据整理、真实到仿真模型适应、基于AMP的强化学习以及仿真到真实部署，在Booster T1机器人上验证了该框架的有效性，并在Booster K1上进行了初步跨平台验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决人形机器人运动学习中因机器人可行运动数据稀缺而导致的任务控制策略难以实现自然且物理可行行为的问题，特别是人类演示与机器人形态不兼容、开源片段质量参差不齐以及仿真轨迹仍需可行性检查等挑战。

Method: 构建一个集成了运动数据整理、真实到仿真模型适应、采用AMP（Adversarial Motion Priors）的强化学习方法以及仿真到真实迁移部署的数据中心化流程，并在实际机器人平台上进行验证。

Result: 该框架在Booster T1机器人上成功实现了自然且物理可行的运动学习，并展示了良好的任务性能；同时在Booster K1机器人上进行了初步的跨平台验证，表明其具有一定的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的以数据为中心的流程有效缓解了人形机器人运动学习中数据稀缺和物理可行性问题，为从仿真到现实的策略迁移提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Humanoid robot motion learning requires not only task-oriented control policies but also physically feasible and natural behaviors that can be transferred to real robots. However, robot-feasible motion data are often scarce: raw human demonstrations may be incompatible with the robot morphology, open-source clips vary in quality, and simulation-collected robot trajectories still require feasibility checking. To address these challenges, we propose a data-centric training and deployment pipeline that integrates motion data curation, real-to-sim model adaptation, AMP-based reinforcement learning, and sim-to-real deployment. We validate the framework on the Booster T1 robot and further provide preliminary cross-platform validation on Booster K1.

</details>


### [146] [PPO-EAL: Exact Augmented Lagrangian Proximal Policy Optimization for Safe Robotic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27861)
*Jiatao Ding,Songqun Gao,Andrea Del Prete,Matteo Saveriano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PPO-EAL的新型约束策略优化框架，将精确增广拉格朗日方法融入近端策略优化，用于安全强化学习控制。该方法在保证任务性能的同时实现了精确的约束满足，并通过动量调节的乘子更新提升了稳定性，在多种机器人任务中表现出优越的安全性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的强化学习方法在处理复杂机器人控制任务时往往忽略安全性要求，而现有安全强化学习算法难以高效地学习策略并精确满足物理约束。因此，需要一种既能高效训练又能严格满足安全约束的方法。

Method: 提出PPO-EAL框架，结合剪切策略更新与精确二次惩罚项，在近端策略优化中引入精确增广拉格朗日法；采用动量调节的乘子更新机制以提升对偶变量稳定性，从而减少约束振荡和不安全行为。

Result: 在多个GPU加速的机器人基准任务（如倒立摆平衡、七自由度机械臂到达、四足行走等）上验证了PPO-EAL的有效性，相比现有先进的一阶安全强化学习方法，展现出更优的安全精度和奖励表现；并在接触密集的齿轮装配任务中实现了零样本的仿真到真实部署，显著提高任务成功率、降低接触力峰值并增强操作鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PPO-EAL是一种通用且可实际部署的安全强化学习框架，适用于各类安全关键的机器人系统，能够在保持高性能的同时实现严格的约束满足。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a promising solution to accomplish complex robotic control tasks; however, most of the current work ignores the safety requirements. Safe RL seeks to maximize task performance while satisfying explicit physical constraints, but current algorithms struggle to learn the policy efficiently with precise constraint satisfaction. This work proposes PPO-EAL, a novel first-order constrained policy optimization framework that integrates exact augmented Lagrangian optimization into proximal policy optimization for safe robotic control. By combining clipped policy updates with exact quadratic penalty terms, PPO-EAL achieves theoretically grounded constraint enforcement without requiring impractically large penalty factors. A momentum-regulated multiplier update further improves dual-variable stability, reducing constraint oscillation and unsafe behavior while preserving task performance. We provide exactness and convergence analysis under standard stochastic approximation assumptions. Extensive validation across diverse GPU-accelerated robotic benchmarks-including cart-pole balancing, cart-double-pendulum stabilization, 7-DoF Franka end-effector reaching, and quadrupedal locomotion-demonstrates superior safety precision and reward performance compared with state-of-the-art first-order safe RL baselines. Finally, we demonstrate zero-shot sim-to-real deployment in a contact-rich gear assembly task, where PPO-EAL substantially improves task success, reduces peak contact force, and enhances operational robustness. These results establish PPO-EAL as a general and practically deployable safe RL framework for diverse safety-critical robotic systems.

</details>


### [147] [LocalNav: Distilling Frontier VLMs and Embodied RL for On-Device Object Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27871)
*Nicolas Baumann,Liam Boyle,Pu Deng,Edoardo Ghignone,Boyang Sun,Marc Pollefeys,Luca Benini,Michele Magno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种知识蒸馏策略，将大型视觉语言模型的空间语义推理能力迁移到轻量级4B参数模型中，实现面向资源受限机器人的低延迟本地化部署，在HM3D OVON基准上显著降低推理延迟的同时保持较高效能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型虽在机器人任务中表现出色，但计算开销大，难以在资源受限的嵌入式设备上实现实时推理，亟需高效的本地化部署方案。

Method: 采用基于场景图的SotA流水线生成前沿推理轨迹，通过知识蒸馏将Claude Sonnet 4.6的推理能力迁移至Qwen3.5-4B模型，并引入E-RLVR与Token生成正则化及量化技术优化输出长度与推理延迟。

Result: 在HM3D OVON基准上，蒸馏后模型达到34.5%的成功率（原为39.7%），Token生成开销减少72.1%，延迟降低71.8%，结合量化累计延迟减少82.8%。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了高性能与低延迟的平衡，为移动机器人上的本地视觉语言模型部署提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) have emerged in the robotic domain as a powerful tool that enables environmental perception with language context, serving as a catalyst for open-vocabulary tasks like ObjectNav. Yet, their computational footprint typically confines them to cloud execution, hindering low-latency inference with local deployment on resource-constrained robots. To address this challenge, we present a distillation strategy that transfers complex spatial-semantic reasoning from large frontier models into a lightweight, 4B-parameter local VLM for edge execution on embedded GPU devices (e.g., Jetson Orin). We first establish a State of the Art (SotA), Scene Graph (SG)-based pipeline using Claude Sonnet 4.6, achieving a 39.7% Success Rate (SR) on the HM3D OVON benchmark. We then demonstrate that fine-tuning Qwen3.5-4B on just 500 frontier reasoning traces effectively enables navigation capabilities, yielding a SR of 34.5%, narrowing the gap to the performance of large cloud models. Finally, we introduce E-RLVR with Token Generation (TG) regularization to compress output sequence lengths for physical deployment while grounding the agent in its task. This downstream optimization reduces TG overhead by 72.1% and latency by 71.8%. Combined with quantization, this joint strategy yields a cumulative 82.8% reduction in overall inference latency without significantly sacrificing performance, presenting a viable paradigm for local, low-latency VLM execution on mobile robots.

</details>


### [148] [S$^2$-VLA: State-Space Guided Vision-Language-Action Models for Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27872)
*Zhipeng Xie,Zongyi Han,Xiangyi Wei,Shiliang Sun,Yang Li,Jing Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: S$^2$-VLA提出了一种状态空间引导的自适应注意力机制（SSGAA），通过动态融合视觉、语言和动作特征，有效缓解了长视野机器人操作任务中的累积误差问题，在较小参数规模下实现了优于更大模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型因采用静态特征融合机制，在长视野任务中存在累积误差传播问题，导致性能下降，难以适应不同任务阶段的需求。

Method: 设计了SSGAA机制，利用维护的信念状态追踪任务进展，并生成动态门控权重来自适应地融合视觉特征、任务意图和时序动作序列三种信息源。

Result: S$^2$-VLA在LIBERO和SimplerEnv等长视野操作基准上达到了最先进的性能，且仅具有2B参数，表现优于7B级模型。

Conclusion: 自适应特征融合对提升长视野机器人操作任务的性能至关重要，动态调整信息融合权重能显著增强模型的任务适应能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong capabilities in robotic manipulation, but their performance degrades significantly in long-horizon tasks due to cumulative error propagation. This limitation largely arises from static feature fusion mechanisms that rely on fixed weights to combine visual, language, and action representations, preventing the model from adapting to different phases of task execution. To address this limitation, we propose S$^2$-VLA, a framework that introduces a State-Space Guided Adaptive Attention (SSGAA) mechanism. SSGAA maintains a belief state that tracks task progression and generates dynamic gating weights to adaptively fuse information from three complementary sources visual features for spatial perception, task intents for high-level task planning, and temporal action sequences for execution consistency. This adaptive fusion allows the model to shift its focus throughout task execution, aligning with the evolving requirements of different task stages. Despite its compact 2B parameter size, S$^2$-VLA consistently outperforms larger 7B-scale models and achieves state-of-the-art performance on long-horizon manipulation benchmarks, including LIBERO and SimplerEnv. highlighting the importance of adaptive feature fusion for long-horizon robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [149] [Swarm sign language: motion-based communication between drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27883)
*Thomas Rey,Julien Moras,Alexandre Eudes,Antoine Manzanera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于运动的视觉通信方法，用于在隐蔽约束下的 swarm 机器人中实现非主动信息交换，通过可动态执行的平面轨迹作为视觉信号，并设计了3DTrajDecoder来解码姿态估计数据，同时进行分类、分割及几何参数回归，系统经过仿真与真实实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在隐蔽任务中，传统的无线通信可能被干扰或探测，因此需要一种不依赖主动发射信号的通信方式；视觉通信利用运动轨迹传递信息，能够在保持低可探测性的同时实现 swarm 机器人之间的信息交换。

Method: 采用模块化、动态可行的平面轨迹作为视觉通信信号；接收端通过姿态估计器获取发送无人机的姿态序列，输入自定义的3DTrajDecoder网络，该网络联合完成轨迹序列的分类、分割以及大小和法向量的回归；为增强训练鲁棒性，构建了一个可配置的在线程序化生成管道以生成多样化轨迹数据。

Result: 系统在真实世界实验和仿真中均得到验证，明确了其适用的操作域；消融实验表明所提出的网络架构和训练策略对性能有显著提升；系统能有效区分通信与非通信轨迹，并准确解码轨迹语义与几何属性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于运动轨迹的视觉通信框架为 stealth-constrained swarm 机器人提供了一种可靠、抗干扰的非主动通信方案，结合程序化数据生成与专用解码器设计，实现了鲁棒的信息解码能力。

Abstract: In stealth-constrained swarm robotics, visual communication provides a critical alternative to active radio transmissions, which might be jammed. This research investigates motion-based communication for non-active information exchange, utilizing modular, dynamically feasible planar trajectories as visual cues. On the receiver drone end, a pose estimator tracks the transmitting drone's pose, feeding it into our custom 3DTrajDecoder. The decoder is designed to classify and segment the spatiotemporal sequence while simultaneously regressing its size and normal vector. To robustly train the decoder on both communicative and non-communicative trajectories, we developed a configurable online procedural generation pipeline. We validate our system through real-world testing and simulation to define its operating domain, supported by an extensive ablation study detailing our architectural choices and system limitations.

</details>


### [150] [Drifting in the Future: Stabilizing Path Following Drifting on High-Latency Vehicle Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27914)
*Frederik Werner,Till Heintzenberg,Markus Lienkamp,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种能够在量产汽车上实现自主漂移的控制器框架，克服了执行器延迟和驱动轴耦合等问题，并通过实验证明了其在真实场景中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动驾驶漂移技术主要依赖于具有瞬时扭矩输出和独立轮毂驱动的研究平台，难以应用于存在执行器延迟和机械耦合的量产车辆。本文旨在解决这些限制，推动自动驾驶控制至车辆稳定性极限之外。

Method: 设计了一个预测器以补偿动力系统延迟，提出了改进的控制架构以适应更高的执行延迟和驱动桥差速器耦合，并引入基于刹车的速度稳定机制。

Result: 在搭载内燃机的量产跑车上实现了稳定的圆形和八字形漂移动作，横向误差控制在1.1米以内，侧滑角超调仅为0.06弧度，即使执行器延迟超过250毫秒仍能保持稳定跟踪并抑制振荡。

Conclusion: 研究表明，自主漂移可在具备传统动力系统的量产车辆上实现，为极端工况下的先进车辆安全控制系统提供了技术基础。

Abstract: Autonomously controlling and handling a vehicle at and beyond its stability limit is a mathematically and computationally demanding task. Prior demonstrations of automated drifting have been limited to research platforms with instantaneous torque delivery and independently actuated wheels, leaving their applicability to production vehicles with actuator latencies and mechanically coupled axles uncertain. To overcome these issues, we design a predictor to compensate for powertrain delays, develop a revised control formulation to accommodate higher actuation latencies as well as a differential coupling on the driven axle, and introduce brake-based velocity stabilization. This paper presents the controller framework, the model extensions, and real-world experimental results.
